2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis project demonstrates a complete Continuous Integration and Continuous Deployment (CI/CD) pipeline for a Python Flask application using \u003cstrong\u003eGitHub Actions\u003c/strong\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhenever code is pushed to the \u003cstrong\u003emain\u003c/strong\u003e branch, GitHub Actions automatically:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eChecks out the source code\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eInstalls Python dependencies\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRuns unit tests using pytest\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eBuilds a Docker image tagged with the Git commit SHA\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePushes the Docker image to Amazon Elastic Container Registry (ECR)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eDeploys the latest image to an Amazon EC2 instance using AWS Systems Manager (SSM)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePerforms a deployment health check\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSends an email notification indicating whether the deployment succeeded or failed\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch1 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"@app.route(\u0026quot;/health\u0026quot;)\ndef health():\n    try:\n        # Verify MongoDB connection\n        mongo.cx.admin.command(\u0026quot;ping\u0026quot;)\n\n        return {\n            \u0026quot;status\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;healthy\u0026quot;,\n            \u0026quot;database\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;connected\u0026quot;\n        }, 200\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        return {\n            \u0026quot;status\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;unhealthy\u0026quot;,\n            \u0026quot;database\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;disconnected\u0026quot;,\n            \u0026quot;error\u0026quot;: str(e)\n        }, 500\n\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e@app.route(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e/health\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e)\ndef \u003cspan health endpoint\u0026quot;\u0026quot;\u0026quot;\n    response = client.get(\u0026quot;/health\u0026quot;)\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    json_data = response.get_json()\n    assert json_data[\u0026quot;status\u0026quot;] == \u0026quot;healthy\u0026quot;\n    assert json_data[\u0026quot;database\u0026quot;] == \u0026quot;connected\u0026quot;\n\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003edef test_health_endpoint(client):\n    \"\"\"Test health endpoint\"\"\"\n    response = client.get(\"/health\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    json_data = response.get_json()\n    assert json_data[\"status\"] == \"healthy\"\n    assert json_data[\"database\"] == \"connected\"\n\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Project Overview</h2><a id="user-content-project-overview" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Project Overview" href="#project-overview"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This project demonstrates a complete Continuous Integration and Continuous Deployment (CI/CD) pipeline for a Python Flask application using <strong>GitHub Actions</strong>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Whenever code is pushed to the <strong>main</strong> branch, GitHub Actions automatically:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Checks out the source code</li>
<li>Installs Python dependencies</li>
<li>Runs unit tests using pytest</li>
<li>Builds a Docker image tagged with the Git commit SHA</li>
<li>Pushes the Docker image to Amazon Elastic Container Registry (ECR)</li>
<li>Deploys the latest image to an Amazon EC2 instance using AWS Systems Manager (SSM)</li>
<li>Performs a deployment health check</li>
<li>Sends an email notification indicating whether the deployment succeeded or failed</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Architecture</h1><a id="user-content-architecture" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Architecture" href="#architecture"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="sudo yum update -y

sudo yum install docker -y

sudo systemctl enable docker

sudo systemctl start docker

sudo usermod -aG docker ec2-user

newgrp docker"><pre>sudo yum update -y

sudo yum install docker -y

sudo systemctl <span class="pl-c1">enable</span> docker

sudo systemctl start docker

sudo usermod -aG docker ec2-user

newgrp docker</pre></div>

<p dir="auto">Amazon Linux 2023 already includes the SSM Agent.</p>
<p dir="auto">Verify</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="sudo systemctl status amazon-ssm-agent"><pre>sudo systemctl status amazon-ssm-agent</pre></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Attach IAM Role</h3><a id="user-content-attach-iam-role" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Attach IAM Role" href="#attach-iam-role"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Attach an IAM Role containing</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>AmazonSSMManagedInstanceCore</li>
<li>AmazonEC2ContainerRegistryPowerUser</li>
<li>CloudWatchAgentServerPolicy</li>
</ul>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">Note:
The assignment recommends AmazonEC2ContainerRegistryReadOnly.</p>
<p dir="auto">AmazonEC2ContainerRegistryPowerUser was used because the EC2 instance was also used during testing and image management.</p>
</blockquote>

student_user

student_user

</code></pre></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Network Access</h2><a id="user-content-network-access" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Network Access" href="#network-access"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Allow</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="0.0.0.0/0"><pre class="notranslate"><code>0.0.0.0/0
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">or add the EC2 Public IP.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Obtain Connection String</h2><a id="user-content-obtain-connection-string" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Obtain Connection String" href="#obtain-connection-string"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="mongodb+srv://username:password@student-cluster.mongodb.net/&lt;db_name&gt;?retryWrites=true&amp;w=majority"><pre class="notranslate"><code>mongodb+srv://username:password@student-cluster.mongodb.net/&lt;db_name&gt;?retryWrites=true&amp;w=majority
</code></pre></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Environment Variables</h1><a id="user-content-environment-variables" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Environment Variables" href="#environment-variables"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Create</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=".env"><pre class="notranslate"><code>.env
</code></pre></div>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="MONGO_URI=your_mongodb_connection_string

SECRET_KEY=your_secret_key"><pre class="notranslate"><code>MONGO_URI=your_mongodb_connection_string

SECRET_KEY=your_secret_key
</code></pre></div>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Run Project Locally</h1><a id="user-content-run-project-locally" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Run Project Locally" href="#run-project-locally"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Clone</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="git clone &lt;repository-url&gt;

cd flask_Practice"><pre>git clone <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>repository-url<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>

<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> flask_Practice</pre></div>
<hr>
<p dir="auto">Create Virtual Environment</p>
<p dir="auto">Windows</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="python -m venv venv

venv\Scripts\activate"><pre>python -m venv venv

venv<span class="pl-cce">\S</span>cripts<span class="pl-cce">\a</span>ctivate</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Linux</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="python3 -m venv venv

source venv/bin/activate"><pre>python3 -m venv venv

<span class="pl-c1">source</span> venv/bin/activate</pre></div>
<hr>
<p dir="auto">Install Dependencies</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pip install -r requirements.txt"><pre>pip install -r requirements.txt</pre></div>
<hr>

<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@app.route(&quot;/health&quot;)
def health():
    try:
        # Verify MongoDB connection
        mongo.cx.admin.command(&quot;ping&quot;)

        return {
            &quot;status&quot;: &quot;healthy&quot;,
            &quot;database&quot;: &quot;connected&quot;
        }, 200

    except Exception as e:
        return {
            &quot;status&quot;: &quot;unhealthy&quot;,
            &quot;database&quot;: &quot;disconnected&quot;,
            &quot;error&quot;: str(e)
        }, 500
"><pre>@app.route(<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>/health<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>)
def <span class="pl-en">health</span>():
    try:
        <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Verify MongoDB connection</span>
        mongo.cx.admin.command(<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>ping<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>)

        <span class="pl-k">return</span> {
            <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>status<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>healthy<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
            <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>database<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>connected<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
        }, 200

    except Exception as e:
        <span class="pl-k">return</span> {
            <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>status<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>unhealthy<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
            <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>database<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>disconnected<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
            <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>error<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>: str(e)
        }, 500
</pre></div>

    &quot;&quot;&quot;Test health endpoint&quot;&quot;&quot;
    response = client.get(&quot;/health&quot;)
    assert response.status_code == 200

    assert json_data[&quot;database&quot;] == &quot;connected&quot;
"><pre class="notranslate"><code>def test_health_endpoint(client):
    """Test health endpoint"""
    response = client.get("/health")
    assert response.status_code == 200
    json_data = response.get_json()
    assert json_data["status"] == "healthy"
    assert json_data["database"] == "connected"

</code></pre></div>

<p dir="auto">Runs</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pytest"><pre>pytest</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">If any test fails</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Docker Image is NOT built</li>
<li>Image is NOT pushed</li>
<li>Deployment does NOT occur</li>
</ul>
<hr>

</code></pre></div>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Deploy</h2><a id="user-content-deploy" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Deploy" href="#deploy"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Deployment is performed using</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="AWS Systems Manager (SSM)"><pre class="notranslate"><code>AWS Systems Manager (SSM)
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Deployment Steps</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Login to ECR</li>
<li>Pull latest Docker Image</li>
<li>Stop old container</li>
<li>Remove old container</li>
<li>Start new container</li>
<li>Pass environment variables</li>
<li>Verify application</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Why SSM instead of SSH?</h1><a id="user-content-why-ssm-instead-of-ssh" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Why SSM instead of SSH?" href="#why-ssm-instead-of-ssh"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Deployment uses AWS Systems Manager (SSM) because</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>No SSH keys required</li>
<li>No inbound port 22 required</li>
<li>More secure</li>
<li>Fully managed by AWS</li>
<li>Suitable for production deployments</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Health Check</h1><a id="user-content-health-check" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Health Check" href="#health-check"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Pipeline verifies deployment using</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="GET /health"><pre class="notranslate"><code>GET /health
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Only when the endpoint returns success is the deployment considered successful.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Email Notification</h1><a id="user-content-email-notification" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Email Notification" href="#email-notification"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Pipeline sends an email after every execution.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Success Email</h2><a id="user-content-success-email" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Success Email" href="#success-email"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Contains</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Build Status</li>
<li>Branch</li>
<li>Commit SHA</li>
<li>Docker Image Tag</li>

<p dir="auto">Login to ECR</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="aws ecr get-login-password --region ap-south-1 \
| docker login \
--username AWS \
--password-stdin &lt;account&gt;.dkr.ecr.ap-south-1.amazonaws.com"><pre>aws ecr get-login-password --region ap-south-1 \
<span class="pl-k">|</span> docker login \
--username AWS \
--password-stdin <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>account<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>.dkr.ecr.ap-south-1.amazonaws.com</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Pull Image</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker pull IMAGE_URI"><pre>docker pull IMAGE_URI</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Stop Existing Container</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker stop flask-app"><pre>docker stop flask-app</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Remove Container</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker rm flask-app"><pre>docker rm flask-app</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Run New Container</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker run -d \
--name flask-app \
-p 5000:5000 \
-e MONGO_URI=&quot;&lt;mongodb-uri&gt;&quot; \
-e SECRET_KEY=&quot;&lt;secret&gt;&quot; \
IMAGE_URI"><pre>docker run -d \
--name flask-app \
-p 5000:5000 \
-e MONGO_URI=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>&lt;mongodb-uri&gt;<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> \
-e SECRET_KEY=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>&lt;secret&gt;<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> \
IMAGE_URI</pre></div>
